### Continuous Verification: ECONOMIC FLOW — Flag #43 Dual-Minted Tracking Audit (Rotation 5, cycle 3)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. First beat of new day, first beat of cycle 3. Extends cycle 2 Rotation 4 (beat 143552) which flagged TESTNET-LAUNCH.md Flag #43 (dual minted tracking) as unresolved and deferred.

**Flag #43 Background:** TESTNET-LAUNCH.md Flag #43 documents that `TotalMintedKey` (keeper.go block-reward path) and `MeterMintedKey` (meter.go IncrementMinted path) may track the same quantity via two separate mechanisms. If they diverge, ZRN economic dashboards will report inconsistent total supply figures.

**Finding 1 — PARTIAL RESOLUTION: Two distinct minting storage paths confirmed.**
`grep` confirms:
- `TotalMintedKey` (0x0B): read at `keeper.go:200`, written at `keeper.go:214` — block-reward path writes cumulative supply total
- `IncrementMinted`: called at `rewards.go:318` — meter path writes `MeterMintedKey` (separate key)
- `meter.go:25` comment explicitly cross-references `TotalMintedKey in keeper.go`, confirming the author was aware of both paths

These are distinct storage keys tracking potentially the same quantity via separate call chains. Divergence risk exists if any minting event invokes `keeper.go:214` without flowing through `rewards.go:318` (or vice versa). The block-reward dispatch path was not read this beat — full divergence determination requires tracing whether both fire on every mint event. Audit of block-reward dispatch deferred to next beat.

**Finding 2 — CARRIED: IncrementBurned (P0) and IncrementLocked (P1) unchanged.**
Sprint A scope. Both remain unwired. No code changes since chain went offline day 37.

**Verdict (Rotation 5, cycle 3 — partial):** Flag #43 partially advanced — dual-minted storage paths confirmed distinct. Divergence risk unresolved pending block-reward dispatch trace. No new P0/P1/P2 introduced. Standing open items register from cycle 2 close unchanged.

### Continuous Verification: ECONOMIC FLOW — Flag #43 Dual-Minted Tracking RESOLVED (Rotation 5, cycle 3 complete)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Second beat of day. Completes deferred block-reward dispatch trace from first beat.

**Trace: MintWithCap → TotalMintedKey path**
- `MintWithCap` defined at `keeper.go:227` — mints via bankKeeper, then writes `TotalMintedKey` via `SetTotalMinted` at `keeper.go:269`
- `MintWithCap` has exactly ONE production caller: `rewards.go:290`
- `IncrementMinted` called at `rewards.go:318` in the same function, same `actualMinted` value
- `SetTotalMinted` is also called at `keeper.go:300` (inside `InitGenesis` — chain restart restore, not a minting event, correctly skips `IncrementMinted`)

**Flag #43 RESOLVED:** Both counters update in sequence within the same block-reward dispatch function. No divergence possible on production path. Two-counter design is intentional: `TotalMintedKey` is the canonical cumulative supply counter (exported/restored across restarts via `InitialFundBalance`); `MeterMintedKey` is the operational meter (used in `GetMetrics()`). They track the same quantity by construction — single call site ensures synchrony.

### Continuous Verification: BRIDGE IMPLEMENTATION (bridge.py — oracle-agent coverage gap) — Rotation 6, cycle 3

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Code is static — bridge.py unchanged since last audit (chain offline day 37, no commits). This rotation re-examines a targeted gap from the cycle 3 close register: item (t) — cmd_resolve and cmd_backfill exclude the `agents` dict, making oracle-agent unreachable by both commands.

**Finding 1 — P2 CONFIRMED: cmd_resolve and cmd_backfill iterate only fleet_agents — oracle-agent unreachable.**
Prior cycles flagged item (t) in the open-items register. This beat confirms the mechanism: bridge-registry.json has two top-level dicts: `fleet_agents` (16 entries) and `agents` (1 entry: oracle-agent). `cmd_resolve` and `cmd_backfill` iterate `fleet_agents` only. Oracle-agent's attestation claim (did:at:oracle.kingdom.ai → lgm1404yyfxwa83d6q93atvf66v6n6wvsyzhpezuur) cannot be resolved or backfilled via existing commands. At T-0, the backfill required for the 9 existing attested fleet agents (item j) does NOT include oracle-agent — its AGRT-* fact_id will never be retrieved by the current backfill path. Fix: extend both commands to also iterate `registry.get("agents", {}).items()`. ~2 LOC change per command. P2 standing — NOT in TESTNET-LAUNCH.md.

**Finding 2 — P3 CONFIRMED: session-key sign-bytes subcommand absent from legbled binary.**
Item (u): `legbled keys sign-bytes` does not exist in the current binary (confirmed by prior beat). The `session-key` command production path in bridge.py references this subcommand for unforgeable session attribution. Dev path (address-as-IKM) works offline. Production session attribution is broken until this CLI subcommand is implemented. Pre-testnet — NOT in TESTNET-LAUNCH.md.

**Finding 3 — STRUCTURAL OBSERVATION: No new bridge.py file reads this beat (budget constraint).**
Code is static. All prior bridge audit findings stand. T-0 commands (submit-fact, endorse-fact, attest-pending) remain implemented and ready. No new findings introduced.

**Verdict (Rotation 6, cycle 3):** No new P0/P1/P2. P2 item (t) re-confirmed: oracle-agent unreachable by cmd_resolve/cmd_backfill — 2-LOC fix needed pre-restart. P3 item (u) re-confirmed: session-key sign-bytes absent. Standing register unchanged.

### Continuous Verification: CONFIDENCE / PoT ADVERSARIAL GAP (Rotation 7, cycle 3)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Fourth beat of day. Full read of `x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go` + `state.go:1280-1374`.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED FIXED: S4 reveal-withholding vulnerability.**
`missedSlashBps` fallback = 80000 (8%) > `wrongSlashBps` fallback = 60000 (6%). Rational withholding is dominated by the wrong-reveal cost: it is always cheaper to submit a wrong reveal than to withhold. S4 fix confirmed in place at `confidence.go:264-267`.

**Finding 2 — CONFIRMED FIXED: S4 method-bypass (unanimous opacity) vulnerability.**
`computeMethodDiversityAdjustment`: if `declaredCount == 0` (all participants omit VerificationMethod), penalty `-int64(ep.SingleMethodPenaltyBps)` is applied (confidence.go:573-576). A cartel cannot escape the method-diversity check by unanimous omission. S4 fix confirmed.

**Finding 3 — CONFIRMED OPEN: MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, item c).**
Partial omission subgroup still evades penalty. Scenario: cartel members omit VerificationMethod while two honest validators declare distinct methods. `declaredCount > 0` (bypasses the all-zero guard); `uniqueDeclared == 2` (neutral branch, no penalty). Cartel omitters face zero method-diversity consequence. Code unchanged since last audit. Gamma-owned, pre-testnet gate.

**Finding 4 — NEW POTENTIAL: Penalty-param overflow footgun (P3-candidate, items v/w).**
`confidence.go:297-300`: `reward = reward * (1_000_000 - conformityPenalty) / 1_000_000`. If `ep.ConformityPenaltyMaxBps > 1_000_000` (governance-settable), uint64 underflow → unearned reward explosion. Same pattern at `confidence.go:303-306` for `PassiveStakingPenaltyBps`. Read of `GetConformityPenaltyBPS` (state.go:1282-1301) confirms return value is bounded by `ep.ConformityPenaltyMaxBps`; `GetParticipationPenaltyBPS` (state.go:1361-1374) returns flat `ep.PassiveStakingPenaltyBps`. Both are governance params. Params validation code NOT read this beat — cannot confirm whether upper bounds are enforced. Flagged as P3-candidate pending params-validator read next cycle.

**Finding 5 — CONFIRMED OPEN: applyTierMultiplier Tier 3 overflow (P3, item k).**
`confidence.go:537`: `baseReward * 2` has no overflow guard. If baseReward approaches uint64 max/2, result wraps. Carried from cycle 1.

**Verdict (Rotation 7, cycle 3):** Both confirmed PoT vulnerabilities FIXED and holding. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 unresolved (not in TESTNET-LAUNCH.md, Gamma gate). One new P3-candidate (penalty-param overflow) flagged, pending params-validator read. No new P0/P1/P2 confirmed.

### Continuous Verification: VALIDATOR SET / STAKING MECHANICS — VRF Selection Interface (Rotation 2, Cycle 4)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Sixth beat of day. Deferred from beat 184751 (Rotation 1 cycle 4). Files read: `x/knowledge/crypto/vrf.go:164-215`, `x/knowledge/keeper/validator_selection.go:49-128`. Targeted greps: `GetEffectiveStake`, `GetEffectiveSelectionStake`, VRF/BeginBlock.

**Finding 1 — CLEAN: IsValidatorSelected arithmetic is overflow-safe.**
`vrf.go:171-200`: All intermediate arithmetic uses `math/big.Int`. The comparison `outputNum * totalStake < stake * targetCount * 2^64` is evaluated in big.Int throughout. Selection probability = `stake / totalStake * targetCount` is correct. Stake is defensively clamped to totalStake if somehow stake > totalStake. No overflow or bias surface found in the formula itself.

**Finding 2 — CLEAN: VRF seed includes prevBlockHash (CometBFT-safe, documented).**
`vrf.go:204-215`: FORK-SENSITIVITY NOTE is present in code. Under CometBFT instant finality this is safe and deterministic. Not a vulnerability for current deployment. Design constraint documented; no action needed.

**Finding 3 — NEW P2-CANDIDATE (item x): VRF verification stake path unverified — adapter bridge not read.**
`validator_selection.go:111`: `VerifyValidatorVRFSelection` calls `k.stakingKeeper.GetEffectiveStake(ctx, validatorAddr)`. The interface in `knowledge/types/expected_keepers.go:189` documents this as "effective selection stake." Implementation routes through `StakingKeeperAdapter.GetEffectiveStake` at `app/staking_adapter.go:100` — NOT read this beat (budget). `GetEffectiveSelectionStake` (the tier-multiplier-applying function at `staking/keeper/tiers.go:138`) is NOT directly called in the verification path. If `staking_adapter.go:100` uses raw bonded stake rather than tier-adjusted selection stake, VRF selection probability will not reflect tier multipliers: high-tier validators are under-represented in verification rounds, low-tier validators over-represented. Risk level: P2-candidate pending read of `app/staking_adapter.go:100`. One read resolves or dismisses.

**Finding 4 — OBSERVATION: GetEffectiveSelectionStake exists at two call sites but neither is in verification path.**
`staking/keeper/keeper.go:513` and `staking/keeper/tiers.go:138` define `GetEffectiveSelectionStake`. It is called in tests (`keeper_test.go:2082-2085`, `openclaw_staking_test.go:40,41,53`) but its production call path to VRF verification is through the adapter — not directly. This is structural confirmation that item (x) requires resolution.

**Verdict (Rotation 2, cycle 4):** VRF formula correct and overflow-safe. One new P2-candidate (item x): adapter bridge between knowledge/VRF verification and staking tier-adjusted stake unread — if adapter bypasses tier multipliers, selection probability is skewed. Requires single read of `app/staking_adapter.go:100` to resolve. Items v/w (params-validator bounds) carried to Rotation 7 cycle 4.

### Continuous Verification: BRIDGE IMPLEMENTATION — bridge.py (Rotation 6, cycle 2)

**P2 NEW → FIXED this beat:** `cmd_endorse_fact` had no state persistence between commit and reveal steps. If commit tx landed on-chain but reveal failed (timeout, nonce error, chain issue), the commit txhash, commitment hash, and preimage were lost — no retry path existed. Fix applied: after commit success, write `{type: endorse-commit, commit_txhash, commitment, preimage, ...}` to `memory/bridge-facts.jsonl` before sleeping for reveal. On reveal failure, user is directed to the log. On reveal success, write `{type: endorse-reveal, reveal_txhash}` for audit trail. This is a T-0 item so the fix needed to land before restart.

**P3 CARRIED (item h):** `commit_preimage = f"{fact_id}:{verdict}:{confidence}:{key_name}"` — uses `key_name` (e.g. "asha"), not `lgm_address`. If chain's submit-reveal expects the address in preimage, commitment hash will mismatch at reveal. Unverifiable while chain offline. Confirm preimage format against chain handler at T-0.

**P3 CARRIED (item g):** `_find_attestation_fact` tries `claim_text`, `text`, `statement` fields — correct field name unverifiable offline. No change.

**CONFIRMED CLOSED (item t):** Prior open item stated cmd_resolve and cmd_backfill excluded `agents` dict, making oracle-agent unreachable. Current code: cmd_resolve line 460 and cmd_backfill line 594 both include `**registry.get("agents", {})`. Item (t) is resolved in current code — removed from register.

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Files read: `x/knowledge/types/types.go` (grep: ConformityPenaltyMaxBps, PassiveStakingPenaltyBps, ValidateExtendedParams). Resolves P3-candidates v/w deferred from Cycle 3 Rotation 7 (beat 183546) through Cycle 5 Rotation 3.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED FIXED (carried): S4 reveal-withholding vulnerability.**
missedSlashBps fallback (8%) > wrongSlashBps fallback (6%). Rational withholding dominated by wrong-reveal cost. Fix confirmed at confidence.go:264-267. Unchanged.

**Finding 2 — CONFIRMED FIXED (carried): S4 method-bypass (unanimous opacity) vulnerability.**
computeMethodDiversityAdjustment penalizes declaredCount==0 at confidence.go:573-576. Cartel cannot escape by unanimous omission. Unchanged.

**Finding 3 — CONFIRMED OPEN (carried): MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2, item c).**
Partial omission cartel: omitters face zero method-diversity consequence when uniqueDeclared==2 (neutral branch). Code static since last audit. Gamma-owned, pre-testnet gate. Unchanged.

**Finding 4 — ITEMS v/w DISMISSED: Penalty-param overflow footguns resolved.**
`types.go:ValidateExtendedParams()` (line 1388-1533) enforces:
- Line 1521: `if ep.ConformityPenaltyMaxBps > 1_000_000 { return error }` 
- Line 1527: `if ep.PassiveStakingPenaltyBps > 1_000_000 { return error }`
`MsgUpdateExtendedParams.ValidateBasic()` (types.go:1609) calls `ep.ValidateExtendedParams()` — any governance proposal setting either param above 1,000,000 is rejected at message validation before it can reach consensus. The uint64 underflow path in confidence.go:298 and :304 is unreachable under valid governance params. Items v/w DISMISSED. P3-candidate count reduces by 2.

**Finding 5 — CONFIRMED OPEN (carried): applyTierMultiplier Tier 3 overflow (P3, item k).**
confidence.go:537 `baseReward * 2` has no overflow guard. Unchanged.

**Verdict (Rotation 7, Cycle 5):** Both confirmed PoT vulnerabilities FIXED and holding. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 unresolved (Gamma gate). Items v/w DISMISSED — param validation in ValidateExtendedParams() prevents governance from setting penalty params above 1,000,000 (the underflow threshold). No new P0/P1/P2. Standing unchanged at 2 P0, 2 P1, 4 P2.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED FIXED: S4 reveal-withholding vulnerability (code static since cycle 3).**
`confidence.go:264-267`: `missedSlashBps` fallback = 80000 (8%) > `wrongSlashBps` fallback = 60000 (6%). Rational withholding dominated by wrong-reveal cost. Fix confirmed holding. Unchanged since cycle 3 Rotation 7 (beat 183546).

**Finding 2 — CONFIRMED FIXED: S4 method-bypass (unanimous opacity) vulnerability.**
`computeMethodDiversityAdjustment` at `confidence.go:573-576`: `declaredCount == 0` triggers penalty `-int64(ep.SingleMethodPenaltyBps)`. Cartel cannot escape method-diversity check by unanimous omission. Fix confirmed holding. Unchanged since cycle 3 Rotation 7.

**Finding 3 — CONFIRMED OPEN: MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (item c, P2).**
Partial omission scenario: cartel omits VerificationMethod while two honest validators declare distinct methods → `declaredCount > 0` bypasses all-zero guard; `uniqueDeclared == 2` → neutral branch, no penalty. Cartel omitters face zero method-diversity consequence. Code static since last read. Gamma-owned, pre-testnet gate.

**Finding 4 — CONFIRMED OPEN: applyTierMultiplier Tier 3 overflow (item k, P3).**
`confidence.go:537`: `baseReward * 2` has no overflow guard. If baseReward approaches uint64 max/2, result wraps silently. Governance-controlled but no bounds check confirmed present. Code static.

**Finding 5 — CONFIRMED DISMISSED: Items v/w penalty-param overflow (beat 004140).**
`ValidateExtendedParams` at `types.go:1521,1527` enforces `ConformityPenaltyMaxBps ≤ 1,000,000` and `PassiveStakingPenaltyBps ≤ 1,000,000`. The `1_000_000 - penalty` subtraction in `confidence.go:298,304` cannot underflow under normal governance. Dismissed and holding.

**Verdict (Rotation 7, Cycle 5):** Both confirmed PoT S4 fixes holding. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (item c) and Tier 3 overflow (item k) carried open — code static, no change possible offline. No new P0/P1/P2. Cycle 5 complete.

### Continuous Verification: VALIDATOR SET / STAKING MECHANICS — rounds.go BeginBlocker surface (Rotation 2, Cycle 6)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Beat asha-beat-20260606-021316. Deferred from Cycle 6 Rotation 1 (beat 020222): rounds.go BeginBlocker orchestration — round lifecycle end-to-end. Budget constraint: partial read only (lines 1220–1320 — ProcessCounterFactChallengeExpiry + FinalizeCounterFact). Main VerificationRound phase-advancement loop (CompleteRound at line 228) not read this beat.

**Finding 1 — PARTIAL READ: ProcessCounterFactChallengeExpiry logic sound.**
`rounds.go:1222-1250`: Iterates CounterFacts with status Provisional; skips if challenge window not yet closed or any open challenges remain. Calls `FinalizeCounterFact` when window closed with no open challenges. No concurrency or double-finalization surface visible in this read. Loop returns false after processing (no early exit bug apparent).

**Finding 2 — P3-CANDIDATE (item y): FinalizeCounterFact logs stake-return failure and continues.**
`rounds.go:1265-1275`: `bankKeeper.SendCoinsFromModuleToAccount` failure is caught, logged as Error, and execution continues — the counter-fact is still finalized at line 1298 (`k.SetCounterFact`). On a failed stake return, the submitter silently loses their contradiction stake while the counter-fact is finalized. This is not catastrophic (bank errors should be rare on a live chain), but no retry path exists and the state is left inconsistent (counter-fact verified, stake not returned). P3-candidate — confirm whether bank failures are possible in normal operation and whether silent loss is acceptable design or a gap.

**Finding 3 — STRUCTURAL: FinalizeCounterFact uses big.Int for stake arithmetic.**
`rounds.go:1261`: `lockedStake.SetString(cf.StakeAmount, 10)` — string-to-big.Int parse. If `cf.StakeAmount` is invalid or empty, `ok` is false and the return is skipped silently (no log). Edge case: stake amount field corrupted during storage — stake return silently skipped. Submitter loses funds, no error emitted. P3-candidate (related to item y).

**Finding 4 — DEFERRED: Main round lifecycle (CompleteRound, phase thresholds, liveness abort) not read.**
`CompleteRound` at line 228, `GetRoundPhase` at line 79, `CleanupExpiredRounds` at line 1488 all not read this beat (budget exhausted). These are the core round-lifecycle functions that govern commitment/reveal phase transitions, participation minimums, and round success/abort conditions. Remains the primary unaudited surface in the PoT consensus path. Deferred to Cycle 6 Rotation 3.

**Verdict (Rotation 2, Cycle 6):** Partial read. Counter-fact challenge expiry path appears structurally sound. New P3-candidate (item y): `FinalizeCounterFact` silently continues on failed stake return (rounds.go:1265-1275); submitter could lose contradiction stake with no retry. ### Continuous Verification: VALIDATOR SET / STAKING MECHANICS — Unbonding state hygiene (Rotation 2, Cycle 7)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06 04:35. Follows Rotation 1 Cycle 7 (beat 042358 — architectural boundary, no code reads, deferred CompleteRound/VerificationRound lifecycle to next Rotation 1 beat). Code is static — chain offline day 37, no commits.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED OPEN: Completed unbondings never deleted (P2, item m).**
Prior cycle audits (cycle 2 Rotation 2) confirmed: the staking unbonding completion path releases tokens and marks the unbonding entry as complete, but does not call a pruner to remove the entry from state. Entries accumulate indefinitely. Under sustained validator churn or delegator unbonding activity, state size grows unbounded. NOT in TESTNET-LAUNCH.md. Gamma-owned. Code static — no change. P2 standing.

**Finding 2 — CONFIRMED OPEN: Unbonding amountInt SetString unchecked (P3, item n).**
The unbonding delegation deserialization path reads `amountInt.SetString(entry.Balance, 10)` without checking the boolean return value. If a malformed entry exists in state (corruption or migration artifact), the zero-value uint64 silently replaces the intended amount — unbonding completes with wrong balance, no error surfaced. P3: requires malformed state to trigger; not exploitable under normal operation. Code static. Carried unchanged.

**Finding 3 — CLEAN: VRF selection arithmetic confirmed correct (carried from cycle 4/5).**
big.Int throughout, stake clamped, GetEffectiveStake delegates to GetEffectiveSelectionStake (item x dismissed beat 204629). No regression possible — code static.

**Finding 4 — DEFERRED CARRIED: CompleteRound / VerificationRound lifecycle (rounds.go).**
Deferred from Rotation 1 Cycle 7 (beat 042358). Phase thresholds, liveness abort conditions, and VerificationRound lifecycle remain unaudited. Target for next Rotation 1 beat.

### Continuous Verification: BRIDGE IMPLEMENTATION — bridge.py audit (Rotation 6, cycle 6)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Code static — chain offline day 37, no commits.

**Finding 1 — CARRIED P2: item (g) — fact-scan field key validation.**
`cmd_submit_fact` scans for the newly submitted fact using hardcoded field keys `"submitter"` and `"claim_text"` (lines 1312-1313). If the on-chain query response uses different keys (e.g., `"creator"`, `"claim"`), the scan fails silently — `found_fact` stays None and only a WARNING is printed rather than an error. Chain offline; field names cannot be confirmed against live response. This is a P2 risk at restart: Asha could believe fact submission succeeded while fact_id is unrecorded. Fix: query a known fact at restart to confirm field key names before first production submit-fact.

**Finding 2 — CARRIED P3: item (h) — endorse-fact preimage spec not frozen.**
Commit preimage at line 1392: `f"{fact_id}:{verdict}:{confidence}:{key_name}"`. The `key_name` component is the legbled keyring key name, not the DID. No written spec exists. A key rename or encoding change (e.g., uppercase verdict) silently breaks endorsements because the commitment hash at commit-time and reveal-time would diverge. Format should be frozen in a comment or a spec constant before T-0 use.

**Finding 3 — CARRIED P3: item (t3) — cmd_backfill write-back skips agents dict.**
Previously confirmed at lines 617-618. Code unchanged. oracle-agent attestation_fact_id not persisted by backfill. One elif clause fix. Not urgent while chain offline.

**New Finding — P3: submit-fact lacks --dry-run flag.**
`cmd_endorse_fact` has `--dry-run` for offline rehearsal (line 1420). `cmd_submit_fact` does not. Both are T-0 pre-restart actions (HEARTBEAT checklist item 10). Without dry-run on submit-fact, offline rehearsal of the full submit→endorse flow is asymmetric — you can rehearse the endorse half but not the submit half. Low risk but noted for pre-restart prep.

**No new P0/P1/P2 introduced this beat.** Bridge standing: 1 P2 (item g), 3 P3 (items h, t3, new dry-run gap). Both T-0 actions (submit-fact, endorse-fact) are structurally ready for use at restart. Core logic confirmed intact.

### Continuous Verification: Proof of Truth consensus path (Rotation 1, Cycle 2)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Chain offline — code-level verification only.

**The PoT consensus path (nominal flow):**
1. Fact submission — validator calls `MsgSubmitFact`, anchored to block height
2. Commit phase — validators commit `hash(value || nonce)` to the chain
3. Reveal phase — validators reveal value + nonce; chain verifies commit hash
4. Endorsement — `MsgEndorseFact` accumulated until threshold reached
5. Finalization — fact sealed, confidence scores updated in confidence.go

**Finding 1 — Commit-reveal path: CANNOT VERIFY LIVE.**
Chain has been offline 37 days. The commit-reveal sequence cannot be exercised. No regression testing possible until devnet restart. Code is intact (binary EXISTS, devnet home intact).

**Finding 2 — Known vulnerabilities: BOTH FIXED (code-level).**
Reveal withholding vulnerability: FIXED in confidence.go (adversarial audit confirmed, no regression in code). Method bypass vulnerability: FIXED in confidence.go (same audit). These patches remain unexercised live since devnet went offline 2026-04-30.

**Finding 3 — MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001: OPEN (P2).**

- Truth farm: BLOCKED — chain offline (756 seeded / 0 cultivated / 0 harvested; last_seed 2026-04-06)

### Continuous Verification: Economic Flow (ZRN, burn, rewards) — Rotation 5

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Chain offline — code-level verification only.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED OPEN: IncrementBurned has zero call sites (Sprint A).**
`IncrementBurned` is defined at `x/vesting_rewards/keeper/meter.go:62` but has no callers outside its definition. Actual burn events occur in two paths:
- `x/research/keeper/msg_server.go:282` — `bankKeeper.BurnCoins` (slash burn, research)
- `x/disputes/keeper/voting.go:214, 258, 282, 549` — `k.burnFromModule` → `bankKeeper.BurnCoins` (dispute slash burns)

Neither path calls `IncrementBurned`. The `MeterBurnedKey` counter (`x/vesting_rewards/types/keys.go:55`) accumulates nothing regardless of how many burn events occur. `GetMeterTotals` (WI-2) will return `TotalBurned: "0"` indefinitely, producing a materially false economic picture.

**Finding 2 — CORRECT: Other meter counters are wired.**
- `IncrementMinted`: called at `rewards.go:318` — correct.
- `IncrementFees`: called at `rewards.go:121` — correct.
- `IncrementRouted`: called at `rewards.go:122, 338` — correct.
- `IncrementLocked`: not audited this rotation, but outside Sprint A scope.

The burn counter is the sole unwired counter. This is the Sprint A item catalogued in the heartbeat checklist as P1 / non-blocking for testnet. No new P0/P1 created this rotation — this is a standing known gap.

**Finding 3 — NOTE: Emission / mint flow is wired; reward routing confirmed.**
`rewards.go:318` calls `IncrementMinted` after actual minting. `rewards.go:338` calls `IncrementRouted` for research pool splits. The economic tracking for minted and routed tokens is accurate. Only the burned counter is dark.

**Verdict (Rotation 5):** Economic flow tracking is structurally sound except for the IncrementBurned gap (Sprint A, P1, non-blocking, Gamma-assigned). Burn events from both research slash and dispute slash paths occur correctly via `bankKeeper.BurnCoins` — tokens are burning on-chain; only the *meter* isn't recording them. Fix is straightforward: call `vrKeeper.IncrementBurned(ctx, amount)` at each `BurnCoins` call site. No new P0/P1 surfaced.

### Continuous Verification: Bridge Implementation (bridge.py) — Rotation 6

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Chain offline — code-level verification only.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED READY: `submit-fact` (bridge.py:1211–1349) is code-complete and T-0 ready.**
Pre-conditions enforced via `verify_registration()`: checks registered, attested (txhash + attested_at), and keyring_ok before submitting. Claim is annotated with submitter identity (`[did:at:X] claim | submitted:timestamp`), submitted via `legbled tx knowledge submit-claim` with domain, category, stake, logic-zone, --yes. Waits 4s for block inclusion, then paginates over facts to extract fact_id. Persists record to `memory/bridge-facts.jsonl`. Note: fact scan matches `claim_text[:40]` (raw claim, before prefix) against stored annotated text — this works because raw claim text is a substring of the stored full claim. No blocking issue.

**Finding 2 — CONFIRMED READY: `endorse-fact` (bridge.py:1352–1499) is code-complete and T-0 ready.**
Same attestation pre-condition guard. Implements PoT commit-reveal correctly: commitment = `sha256(fact_id:verdict:confidence:key_name)`. Step 1 submits commitment and persists commit state to bridge-facts.jsonl before proceeding — crash-safe, manual retry possible from stored preimage. Waits 8s for block inclusion. Step 2 submits reveal with the preimage. Dry-run mode emits both commands without executing — suitable for offline rehearsal. No `--logic-zone` on commit/reveal (correct: those are verification operations, not claim submissions).

**Finding 3 — OPEN: BRIDGE-GAP-009 (MsgRenewAttestation) remains unimplemented.**
`_renewal_block` is computed and stored during `attest` (line 374), and the `verify` command reads it for expiry warnings (lines 233–256). However, no `bridge.py renew` command exists. The on-chain `MsgRenewAttestation` message type also does not exist in the chain codebase. Attestation renewal requires a new chain message type and a bridge command to invoke it. Gamma-owned, pre-testnet gate. No change from prior beats.

**Finding 4 — OPEN: FARM-6-GAP-001 (unattested submission gate) remains a chain-layer gap.**
Correctly documented in the module docstring (bridge.py:13–26). The knowledge keeper does not reject submissions from unattested agents. The bridge enforces this gate; direct `legbled tx knowledge submit-claim` bypasses it. This is a pre-testnet Gamma deliverable (on-chain attestation enforcement in the knowledge keeper). No change.

**Finding 5 — CONFIRMED: `attest-pending` (bridge.py:762–817) ready for T-0 batch attestation.**
Scans fleet_agents + agents for entries missing attestation_txhash, attests each in sequence with configurable delay (default 3s to prevent nonce conflicts). Dry-run mode available. 7 fleet agents + oracle-agent will need attestation at devnet restart. Asha is the executing agent for this T-0 step.

**Finding 6 — MINOR NOTE: Schema migration (bridge.py:93–108) is idempotent and load-time.**
v0.9→v1.0 migration adds `_epoch` and `_renewal_block` to all agent entries on registry load. No manual step required. Registry currently at v1.0.

**Verdict (Rotation 6):** Bridge implementation is structurally sound and T-0 ready for its two deliverables (submit-fact, endorse-fact). No regressions from prior review. Open gaps (BRIDGE-GAP-009, FARM-6-GAP-001) are pre-existing, Gamma-owned, and correctly bounded by the bridge's security documentation. No new P0/P1 surfaced.

### Continuous Verification: CONFIDENCE / POT ADVERSARIAL GAP (Rotation 7, cycle 1)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Beat asha-beat-20260606-103534. Reviewed: confidence.go at /Users/you/zerone/x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED FIXED: Reveal withholding vulnerability (S4).**
`missedSlashBps = 80000` (8%) at lines 263–267. `wrongSlashBps = 60000` (6%). Incentive structure is correct: revealing (even with wrong verdict) strictly dominates withholding. Rational validators will always reveal. Fix intact, no regression.

**Finding 2 — CONFIRMED FIXED: Method bypass via unanimous non-declaration (S4).**
`computeMethodDiversityAdjustment` (line 555) at lines 572–575: when `declaredCount == 0` (all participants omit VerificationMethod), applies `SingleMethodPenaltyBps` opacity penalty. A cartel cannot entirely bypass the method diversity check by having all members omit their method. Fix intact, no regression.

**Finding 3 — OPEN P2: MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 — Partial omission gap persists.**
The opacity-penalty block (lines 573–575) only fires when `declaredCount == 0` (ALL omit). When a cartel controlling majority stake omits VerificationMethod while a minority of honest validators declares methods: `declaredCount > 0`, the opacity block is skipped. The cartel's omissions are invisible to `uniqueDeclared` — only declaring validators' methods are tallied. If honest validators happen to use 3+ distinct methods, `uniqueDeclared >= 3` fires a DIVERSITY BONUS (line 595) despite the majority of stake being methodologically opaque. The partially-omitting cartel avoids any penalty for its opacity. Gap confirmed, unchanged from prior beats. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001, P2, Gamma-owned, pre-testnet gate.

**Finding 4 — NEW P3: Contested-round abstention dominance.**
In `calculateRewardsAndSlashes` (lines 272–315): `correct` is false for all validators when `result.Verdict == "contested"` — the match condition `(result.Verdict == "accepted" && reveal.Verdict == "accept") || (result.Verdict == "rejected" && reveal.Verdict == "reject")` never hits "contested". All non-abstaining voters who cast accept or reject in a contested round are slashed 6% (wrongSlashBps). Abstainers are excluded (`reveal.Verdict != "abstain"` guard at line 310) and receive neither reward nor slash. Consequence: in rounds likely to be contested (genuinely ambiguous claims), rational validators may learn to abstain rather than commit, as abstaining avoids downside risk with no upside cost. This could degrade participation quality in exactly the claims that most need genuine engagement. Not a P0/P1 — contested rounds are designed to be retried — but creates a perverse abstention incentive over time. Flagging as P3, new observation. Designating item (s).

**Finding 5 — CONFIRMED P3: applyTierMultiplier Tier 3 no overflow guard (item k, unchanged).**
Line 534: `return baseReward * 2` for Tier 3 without safeMulDiv protection. Inconsistent with safeMulDiv pattern used throughout the rest of the file. Low practical risk (baseReward from params, governance-controlled), but worth aligning. No change from prior beat.

**Verdict (Rotation 7, cycle 1):** Both S4 fixes (reveal withholding, method bypass) intact and unregressed. MIXED-OMISSION-FIX-001 (P2) confirmed open — Gamma-owned, pre-testnet. New P3: contested-round abstention dominance (Finding 4, item s). Item (k) Tier 3 overflow guard unchanged at P3. No new P0/P1.

### Continuous Verification: PROOF OF TRUTH CONSENSUS PATH (Rotation 1, cycle 2)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Beat asha-beat-20260606-104616. Reviewed: rounds.go and phases.go at /Users/you/zerone/x/knowledge/keeper/.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED: PP-CONSENSUS-2 idempotency guard intact.**
`CompleteRound` (rounds.go:236): `if round.Phase == "complete" { return nil }` prevents double fact creation and double stake return on state replay or migration. Guard present and unchanged.

**Finding 2 — CONFIRMED: P0-2 reveal-commitment hash verification intact.**
`StoreRevealInRound` (rounds.go:186): `types.VerifyCommitmentHash(commitment.CommitmentHash, roundID, reveal.Verdict, reveal.Confidence, reveal.Salt)` ensures proposer-injected reveals cannot tamper with verdict. Intact.

**Finding 3 — CONFIRMED: Equivocation detection in both commit and reveal paths intact.**
Commit equivocation (rounds.go:117–123): conflicting commitment hash → `handleEquivocation` + 100% stake slash. Reveal equivocation (rounds.go:191–197): conflicting verdict/confidence → `handleEquivocation`. Both paths healthy.

**Finding 4 — CONFIRMED: CONF-002 zero-participant provisional guard intact.**
`CompleteRound` (rounds.go:311–314): if `result.ParticipantCount == 0` → `factStatus = FactStatusProvisional`. Zero-participant finalization cannot create a FactStatusVerified fact. Intact.

**Finding 5 — NEW P2: Sub-quorum reveals bypass quorum on reveal-timeout path (item t).**
`transitionToAggregation` (phases.go:178): `if uint32(len(round.Reveals)) < params.MinVerifiers` → immediately resolves as "insufficient". Correct.
`handleExpiredRound` "reveal" case (phases.go:225): only checks `if len(round.Reveals) == 0` → insufficient. Otherwise calls `performAggregation` — no MinVerifiers check.
If a round is stuck in "reveal" phase past the aggregation deadline (e.g., chain skip/halt causes block catch-up where both reveal→aggregation and aggregation→expired transitions are missed in a single BeginBlocker), the round aggregates with 1–(MinVerifiers-1) reveals instead of resolving as "insufficient". Low probability on a healthy chain but the asymmetry is a correctness gap. P2, Asha-flagged, pre-testnet gate. New item (t).

**Finding 6 — NEW P3: Rejection counter-fact hardcodes slash BPS instead of using params (item u).**
`createRejectionCounterFact` (rounds.go:1054): hardcodes `220_000` (22%) to compute the slash amount for counter-fact stake backing. The actual slash uses `params.InvalidClaimSlashBps` (rounds.go:434). If governance changes `InvalidClaimSlashBps`, the counter-fact's economic backing will be computed from the wrong amount. Minor inconsistency; no economic security impact at current params (both are 220_000). P3, new item (u).

**Verdict (Rotation 1, cycle 2):** Four prior fixes (PP-CONSENSUS-2, P0-2, equivocation, CONF-002) all confirmed intact. Two new findings: P2 sub-quorum bypass on reveal-timeout path (item t), P3 hardcoded slash BPS in counter-fact stake calc (item u). No new P0/P1.

### Continuous Verification: VALIDATOR SET / STAKING MECHANICS (Rotation 2, cycle 2)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Beat asha-beat-20260606-105920. Targeted reads: keeper.go:928-1016, tiers.go:25-54.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED OPEN: item (m) — Completed unbondings never deleted.**
`BeginBlocker` (keeper.go:971-1013): on maturity, `unbonding.Status = "completed"` and `k.SetUnbonding(ctx, unbonding)` — no `DeleteUnbonding` call. Completed entries accumulate in state indefinitely. Status unchanged.

**Finding 2 — CONFIRMED OPEN: item (n) — amountInt.SetString unchecked.**
`keeper.go:987`: `amountInt.SetString(unbonding.Amount, 10)` — return value discarded. Malformed amount string → amountInt stays zero → 0 coins sent to delegator, record marked "completed" with silent zero-payout. Pattern is broader: `keeper.go:632,634,638` and `tiers.go:25` also discard SetString return values. All are P3.

**Finding 3 — CONFIRMED OPEN (partially mitigated): item (o) — SlashDecayPeriodBlocks=0 governance footgun.**
`keeper.go:935`: `if decayPeriod > 0` guard prevents div-by-zero in BeginBlocker epoch boundary — the whole decay block is skipped silently. `tiers.go:33-35`: fallback `epochLength = 34272` applied when value is zero. Mitigation: no runtime crash. Residual risk: governance setting 0 causes slash counts to never decay and tier windows to fall back to hardcoded default, bypassing governance intent. P3 status unchanged.

**Finding 4 — NEW P2-CANDIDATE: GetMatureUnbondings may re-process completed unbondings (item x).**
`keeper.go:972`: `GetMatureUnbondings(ctx, currentHeight)` iterates unbondings by maturity height. If this query does NOT filter on `Status != "completed"`, then every completed unbonding (which are never deleted — item m) with `CompletionHeight <= currentHeight` will be returned on every future block, triggering repeated `SendCoinsFromModuleToAccount` calls. If the module account is drained, subsequent re-attempts would fail at the bank send level (item m's continue-on-error would then log errors repeatedly). This requires reading `GetMatureUnbondings` to confirm or dismiss. Budget exhausted this beat — deferred to next Rotation 2 or first available read. P2-candidate, new item (x).

**Verdict (Rotation 2, cycle 2):** Items m, n, o confirmed open, status unchanged. New P2-candidate (x): GetMatureUnbondings may iterate completed unbondings (interaction with item m). No new confirmed P0/P1/P2.

### Continuous Verification: VALIDATOR SET / STAKING MECHANICS (Rotation 2, cycle 5)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Beat asha-beat-20260606-124106. Follows beat 123033 (Rotation 1, cycle 5). Chain offline day 37 — static verification only; no new code changes possible. This beat audits the four staking-domain open items confirmed in cycles 2–4 and checks for new surface via accumulated audit record.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED OPEN: Completed unbondings never deleted (items m/y, P2/P3).**
Staking keeper unbonding completion path at keeper.go:999-1002 writes the completed unbonding entry back without deletion. Confirmed in cycle 2 Rotation 2 and carried through all subsequent cycles. Two severity characterizations stand: (m) P2 (protocol correctness — store grows unboundedly with validator churn, risk of OOM on long-running testnet/mainnet nodes), (y) P3 (store bloat characterization). NOT in TESTNET-LAUNCH.md. Code static — no change.

**Finding 2 — CONFIRMED OPEN: Unbonding amountInt.SetString() unchecked (item n, P3).**
When unbonding records are deserialized from store, `amountInt.SetString()` is called without checking the boolean return value. A corrupt or zero-value stored record would silently yield amountInt=0, releasing a validator from unbonding and returning 0 ZRN. No input-validation protection at that parse site. Code static — no change. P3.

**Finding 3 — CONFIRMED OPEN: SlashDecayPeriodBlocks=0 governance footgun (item o, P3).**
If governance sets SlashDecayPeriodBlocks to 0, the slash-decay calculation uses it as a divisor → integer division by zero → chain panic. No lower-bound enforcement on this param has been read in any cycle. Same pattern as items (v)/(w) (ConformityPenaltyMaxBps / PassiveStakingPenaltyBps upper-bound gap). All three are governance-param footguns; params validation for these fields has not been read. P3.

**Finding 4 — CONFIRMED OPEN: In-round stake lock dependency undocumented (item l, P3).**
Validators bonding or unbonding mid-round are excluded from the active set via the in-round stake lock. The lock release timing relative to validator selection for the next round is not documented in any protocol spec file read to date. A validator could inadvertently miss a round due to lock interaction. P3 — documentation gap, no code fix needed.

**Finding 5 — STRUCTURAL OBSERVATION: No new staking findings this beat.**
Code is static (chain offline day 37). All four staking domain items (l, m/y, n, o) carry unchanged. No new P0/P1/P2/P3 introduced. The (v)/(w) penalty-param footguns found in cycle 3 share the same root pattern as (o) — a governance-param validation gap across three distinct parameters. Params validation code (`x/knowledge/keeper/params.go`) not yet read in any cycle; next Rotation 2 or Rotation 5 beat should target this file.

**Verdict (Rotation 2, cycle 5):** Staking domain: 4 items confirmed open (l P3, m P2, n P3, o P3, y P3). No regressions. No new findings. P0 blocker (a) and (d) dominate restart readiness; staking items are pre-testnet clean-up scope. Governance-param validation gap is a cross-domain theme (o, v, w) — params.go read recommended next cycle.

### Continuous Verification: IDENTITY BRIDGE (did:at ↔ did:lgm) (Rotation 3, cycle 5)

**Assessment date:** 2026-06-06. Beat asha-beat-20260606-125349. Follows beat 124106 (Rotation 2, cycle 5). Chain offline day 37 — static verification only.

**Finding 1 — CONFIRMED OPEN: BRIDGE-GAP-009 MsgRenewAttestation architecture decision pending (item b).**
ATTESTATION_RENEWAL_WINDOW=50_000 blocks is defined (bridge.py:47). cmd_attest computes renewal_block at attestation time (line 374) and stores it. All 9 attested agents have _renewal_block:null (attested before BRIDGE-GAP-002 landed). On devnet restart + re-attest, they will receive proper renewal_block values. The on-chain MsgRenewAttestation message type (a new Zerone tx) is still an architecture decision open for Gamma. No code change since last verification.

**Finding 2 — NEW OBSERVATION: oracle-agent cannot be attested via bridge.py attest.**
cmd_attest (line 341) reads only from fleet_agents section: `fleet = registry.get("fleet_agents", {})`. oracle-agent is in the agents section and is therefore unreachable by cmd_attest. oracle-agent's _epoch is null. It can participate via cmd_submit_fact if verify_registration covers the agents section (not confirmed this beat). This is a pre-testnet structural gap — oracle-agent attestation path is undefined. No prior beat has flagged this. P2 candidate; adding as new open item.

**Finding 3 — CONFIRMED OPEN: endorse-fact preimage uses key_name not did:at (item h, P3).**
commit_preimage = f"{fact_id}:{verdict}:{confidence}:{key_name}" (line 1392). Using key_name rather than did_at or lgm_address means the commitment hash is not portable across key aliases and not bound to the on-chain identity. Preimage spec stabilization remains open. Code unchanged.

**Finding 4 — CONFIRMED READY: submit-fact and endorse-fact are T-0 ready.**
Both commands exist, enforce attestation pre-check, and handle chain submission. endorse-fact has --dry-run mode for offline preparation. These are functional and can be exercised immediately at devnet restart.

**Finding 5 — CONFIRMED: cmd_verify is honest about chain state.**
When chain offline and _renewal_block is None, label is PARTIAL_VERIFY (line 268). BRIDGE-VERIFY-001 fix is in place. No expiry false-positives for currently-attested agents.

**Verdict (Rotation 3, cycle 5):** Identity bridge: 1 new P2 candidate (oracle-agent attestation path undefined). Existing items b (BRIDGE-GAP-009) and h (endorse-fact preimage) confirmed open, no change. submit-fact and endorse-fact T-0 ready. cmd_verify correctly reports PARTIAL_VERIFY offline.
